# Project I.G.I. - Newly Discovered Gameplay Natives (Unimplemented in C# OpenIGI)

> **Strict Verification Gate**: Static CFG Score >= 0.85 (`gate == PASS`), strictly absent from C# `open-igi/src/`, and not in baseline 503 catalog.
> **Reference Binary**: Retail `IGI.EXE` (PE32 Intel 80386, Base `0x00400000`, 1,384,448 bytes, SHA256 `dea34892...`).
> **Total Discovered Gameplay Natives**: **2538** across **10 Engine Pillars**.
